# Master Topic Catalogue

Canonical topic records are normalized here so the nine programme files can reuse the same stable topic IDs without duplicating semantics. A placement can appear in several programmes/months while retaining one canonical definition.

- **Unique canonical topics:** 693
- **Total programme placements:** 729
- **Scales:** educational value, popularity, complexity, and hands-on potential use 1–5.

## AI-AGT-001 — Agent Design Patterns: Agent orchestration architecture clinic

- **Track:** Advanced
- **Category:** agents
- **Format:** architecture clinic
- **Description:** An advanced architecture clinic treating single-agent patterns, planners, routers, handoffs, state machines, and when deterministic workflows win as a systems problem. The session focuses on architecture, reliability, scale, trust boundaries, and the assumptions that fail under production or adversarial conditions.
- **Learning outcome:** Design and defend an architecture involving agent design patterns, including reliability, security, scale, and evaluation considerations.
- **Prerequisite IDs:** AI-AGT-055
- **Related IDs:** AI-AGT-028
- **Follow-up IDs:** AI-AGT-025
- **Programmes:** Agentic Systems
- **Months:** October
- **Curriculum status:** Core
- **Voting status:** Recommended
- **Durability:** Durable
- **Estimated educational value:** 5/5
- **Estimated popularity:** 5/5
- **Complexity:** 5/5
- **Hands-on potential:** 3/5

## AI-AGT-028 — Agent Design Patterns: Agent, workflow, or chatbot?

- **Track:** Foundation
- **Category:** agents
- **Format:** presentation
- **Description:** An accessible presentation on single-agent patterns, planners, routers, handoffs, state machines, and when deterministic workflows win. The emphasis is a clear mental model, concrete examples, and the boundaries of the idea rather than framework-specific recipes.
- **Learning outcome:** Explain the core ideas behind agent design patterns, recognize its common failure modes, and decide when the technique is appropriate.
- **Prerequisite IDs:** None
- **Related IDs:** AI-AGT-001, AI-AGT-055
- **Follow-up IDs:** AI-AGT-052, AI-AGT-055

## AI-AGT-055 — Agent Design Patterns: Build an agent with tools

- **Track:** Practitioner
- **Category:** agents
- **Format:** live coding
- **Description:** A practical live coding applying single-agent patterns, planners, routers, handoffs, state machines, and when deterministic workflows win in a working engineering scenario. Participants compare implementation choices, instrument the result, and examine failure modes rather than stopping at a happy-path demo.
- **Learning outcome:** Implement or critique a realistic agent design patterns workflow, make explicit trade-offs, and verify it with tests or measurements.

- **Category:** agents
- **Format:** live coding
- **Description:** A practical live coding applying working memory, durable memory, summaries, retrieval, user state, and memory quality evaluation in a working engineering scenario. - **Track:** Practitioner
- **Category:** architecture
- **Format:** live coding
- **Description:** A practical live coding applying async jobs, queues, persistence, tool execution, rate limits, secrets, and service boundaries around AI workloads in a working engineering scenario.
